- **agent-setup**: `AGENTS.md` is canonical. Every `CLAUDE.md` (root and nested) is a symlink to its sibling `AGENTS.md`: Grok reads only `CLAUDE.md` and ignores `@AGENTS.md` imports, and Claude Code prefers `CLAUDE.md`. `.mcp.json` is the MCP master; `bunx repoagents` regenerates `.codex/config.toml` and `.cursor/mcp.json` and repairs the links (standard: grmkris/personal `infra/agents.md`). The root task map links ([grmkris/goblin](https://github.com/grmkris/goblin/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agent-setup.md))
- **agent-setup**: [`AGENTS.md`](../../AGENTS.md) is the shared instruction source. Codex reads ancestor files down to its working directory; a session at the root must explicitly read the linked workspace instructions before editing a workspace. Every `CLAUDE.md` is a symlink to its sibling `AGENTS.md` (Grok reads only `CLAUDE.md` and ignores `@AGENTS.md` imports); Claude-only notes live in `.claude/rules/claude-co ([grmkris/sidequest](https://github.com/grmkris/sidequest/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agent-setup.md))
- **agent-setup-page-is-configuration-dialog**: Instance setup is not a Card or StandardDialog. The toolkit expects `PageType = ConfigurationDialog` so OK and Cancel are system actions, plus the built-in `Agent Setup Part` for name, display name, state, and access. A Card with custom fields only drops those shared controls and the AI-use notices the part carries. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/agent-setup-page-is-configuration-dialog.md) · ★247 · 7 copies)

- **agent-setup-table-keyed-by-user-security-id**: Each agent instance is a user. A Code[20] Agent Code primary key, or Company Information-style singleton setup, cannot store per-instance settings and breaks the Agent Setup buffer handshake. Use when delegating CI watching to a subagent so the main session can keep working. Requires a PR to already exist — dispatch agent-ship first if there is none. Never merges, never replies to reviews, never edits source; use the ship-autonomous skill in the foreground for those. - **agent-skill-and-mcp**: &gt; 这三个词经常被放在一起比较，但它们根本不在同一个维度上：Agent 是**运行时**，Skill 是**知识包**，MCP 是**接线协议**。它们不竞争，而是三个互相垂直的层。本文从第一性原理拆开这三层，说明各自解决什么问题、边界在哪、以及各自独立的失效模式与安全模型。 ([geekchow/technical-knowledge](https://github.com/geekchow/technical-knowledge/blob/HEAD/ai/agents/agent-skill-and-mcp.md) · ★2)

Invoke this for any "build a document/solution for Entra ID" request. - **agent-tooling**: Claude Code ecosystem engineer. Delegates here for authoring and maintaining Claude Code artifacts — subagents, skills, hooks, slash commands, plugin and marketplace manifests, CLAUDE.md / memory files, and Agent-Context wiring. - **agent_13**: **Key Findings** (bulleted, quantified) - Unit contribution at 79 USD per clinic per month (GIVEN): - Payment processing ~3%: -2.40 USD (ASSUMED) - WhatsApp/SMS reminders: -20 to -30 USD, base -24 USD (ASSUMED: ~800 messages/month at ~0.03 USD) - Incremental hosting and storage: -2 USD (ASSUMED) - Support time at ~1 hour per clinic per month, valued at ~0 USD while founder and engineers absorb it ([Waleed178-del/spm-ultra](https://github.com/Waleed178-del/spm-ultra/blob/HEAD/examples/dental-saas-gcc/agents/agent_13.md))
- **agent_14**: **Key Findings** (bulleted, quantified) - A flat 79 USD per clinic per month (GIVEN) is a workable anchor, but it is mis-shaped: - It charges a solo-dentist clinic the same as a 6-chair clinic, which leaves money on the table from larger clinics. - It bundles an uncapped variable cost (reminders) into a fixed fee. - It ignores the local-currency, VAT-inclusive way Gulf clinics compare prices. Conf ([Waleed178-del/spm-ultra](https://github.com/Waleed178-del/spm-ultra/blob/HEAD/examples/dental-saas-gcc/agents/agent_14.md))
- **agent_15**: **Key Findings** (bulleted, quantified) - Method: - 5-year annual free cash flows, plus a terminal value at a multiple of Year-5 ARR. - Discount rate **40%** (ASSUMED seed-stage venture hurdle rate). - Contribution margin 63% at an ARPA of 79 USD (GIVEN price). - CapEx of 33,000 USD in Year 1. - Fixed costs ~25,000 USD/month in Year 1. - All inputs are ASSUMED unless noted. - **Plan case (300 clin ([Waleed178-del/spm-ultra](https://github.com/Waleed178-del/spm-ultra/blob/HEAD/examples/dental-saas-gcc/agents/agent_15.md))
- **agent_16**: Premise (Klein premortem): it is November 2027. The company has failed: well short of 300 paying clinics, close to out of cash, and the founder is looking for a bridge round that nobody will lead. The causes below are worked back from that outcome and ranked by how much each contributed. ([Waleed178-del/spm-ultra](https://github.com/Waleed178-del/spm-ultra/blob/HEAD/examples/dental-saas-gcc/agents/agent_16.md))
- **agent_17**: Method: ISO 31000 risk identification, analysis and evaluation. Probability (P) is the 12-month likelihood. Impact (I) is on a 1-5 scale, where 5 = the company cannot reach its goal or survive. Score = P x I, with P as a fraction. Tiers: **Catastrophic** = score &gt;=2.5 or I=5 with P&gt;=40%; **Medium** = 1.0-2.5; **Low** = &lt;1.0. ([Waleed178-del/spm-ultra](https://github.com/Waleed178-del/spm-ultra/blob/HEAD/examples/dental-saas-gcc/agents/agent_17.md))

If the MVP slips to month 5, that rises to about 43 a month. - **agent_22**: **Key Findings** - **C4 context level.** The system has four outside dependencies: (1) the WhatsApp Business Platform through Meta or a Business Solution Provider (BSP), (2) an SMS gateway as fallback, (3) a subscription payment gateway, and (4) a cloud host. No PMS or EHR integration is in the MVP. Each dependency is a separate failure domain. WhatsApp is the one the value proposition rests on. * ([Waleed178-del/spm-ultra](https://github.com/Waleed178-del/spm-ultra/blob/HEAD/examples/dental-saas-gcc/agents/agent_22.md))
- **agent_23**: **Key Findings** - **There are three journeys, and each has its own friction budget.** (1) Clinic onboarding: owner or receptionist, once per clinic. (2) Daily reception use: the receptionist, 20-30 bookings a day (ASSUMED). (3) The patient's reminder reply and self-booking: on mobile, often older patients, mostly in Arabic. Journey 3 decides whether no-shows actually fall. **High confidence.** - ([Waleed178-del/spm-ultra](https://github.com/Waleed178-del/spm-ultra/blob/HEAD/examples/dental-saas-gcc/agents/agent_23.md))
- **agent_24**: **Key Findings** - **Load baseline (ASSUMED: 25 appointments per clinic per day, 24 working days a month, 2 reminders per appointment).** ([Waleed178-del/spm-ultra](https://github.com/Waleed178-del/spm-ultra/blob/HEAD/examples/dental-saas-gcc/agents/agent_24.md))
- **agent_25**: **Key Findings** - **Kraljic classification of the vendor stack:** - *Strategic* (high impact, few suppliers): the **WhatsApp Business Platform**. Meta is the only source. BSPs only resell access and add tooling. Lock-in sits with the channel itself, not the BSP. **High confidence.** - *Bottleneck* (lower spend, hard to substitute): **in-country cloud hosting** in KSA, and a **KSA payment gateway* ([Waleed178-del/spm-ultra](https://github.com/Waleed178-del/spm-ultra/blob/HEAD/examples/dental-saas-gcc/agents/agent_25.md))
